Generated by `npm run codemap` from each file's job sentence (docs/codemap/jobs-npc-state-delta.json, the package's docs/dev/codemap.md, or the file's opening comment) and the source. Do not edit by hand. The maps carry no line counts (the 450-line limit is enforced by the size check).

## Feature entry points

| Feature | Start reading at | What it covers |
|---|---|---|
| Extension entry | `index.js` | What SillyTavern loads for NPC State Delta. |
| Start-up | `bootstrap.js` | Starts Delta's runtime and its UI. |
| Core model | `core.js` | NPC records, prompts, calendar and lifecycle rules. |
| Scanning replies | `runtime-scan.js` | Scans replies for NPC changes and updates dossiers. |
| Injection into prompts | `runtime-injection.js` | Adds the present NPCs' notes to the story prompt. |
| Dossier UI | `dossier-ui.js` | The dossier browser, editor and cards. |
| Social graph and relationships | `social.js` | Bonds, facts and the relationship map. |
| Storage | `storage.js` | Per-chat dossier files and their identity. |
| Branches and checkpoints | `branch.js` | Keeps dossiers right across chat branches and rewinds. |
| Bundles | `bundle.js` | Exports and imports dossier bundles. |
| Calendar | `calendar.js` | Story dates, clocks and NPC ages. |
| NPC meters | `src/npc-meters.js` | The Story Block module for NPC meters and stages. |
| Story Block connection | `src/adapter/connect.js` | Finds the Story Block host and registers Delta with it. |

## One job per file: backlog

Files with more than 4 exports (legacy; pure re-export facades are exempt; split when moving out of `packages/`).

- `core-prompts.js`: 23 exports
- `core.js`: 224 exports
- `src/people/view.js`: 5 exports
- `src/storysuite-participant/participant/support.js`: 6 exports
- `src/storysuite-participant/storage-row.js`: 7 exports
- `src/storysuite-participant/timeline.js`: 13 exports
- `src/storysuite-v2.js`: 32 exports
- `storage.js`: 33 exports
